| Chat | This glossary entry is for the UI element that allows user to toggle between different modes: Chat \|Work \|Codex. The translations of this entry should be used in that context exclusively. There is another glossary entry for the general term "chat," which is used across the product.<br><br>Additional context for this glossary entry:<br>ChatGPT and Codex are becoming one app, where a user can switch between three modes through a toggle at the top of the screen, that will look like this:<br><br>   Chat    \|Work   \|Codex<br><br>Chat / Chat mode<br>For questions, learning, and everyday help.<br>Get help with everyday questions and ideas, explaining concepts, drafting messaging, searching the web, generating an image, comparing options, or thinking through a decision.<br><br>Work / Work mode<br>For completing work tasks from start to finish.<br>ChatGPT can create and edit docs, slide decks, spreadsheets, charts, PDFs, images, and other deliverables using the tools, context, and apps you’re already using at work. <br><br>Codex<br>For building, fixing, and understanding code.<br>Codex is now built into ChatGPT, so you can get help creating apps, websites, automations, and software projects. Ask it to produce code, find bugs, and suggest improvements. | noun | — | — | — | Chat |

| Steer | A button used in Codex UI. When the user types new instructions in the composer box, they have two options: Queue or Steer. Queue instructs the model to wait for the current response to complete before working on the response using the new instructions. Steer instructs the model to immediately restart the conversation using the new instructions. | verb | — | — | — | Redirecionar |
